- **analogical-reasoning**: 类比推理——当法律对当前问题没有直接规定时，通过相似案件/规范的类推适用填补法律漏洞。 适用情形：新型案件缺乏直接规定、需要"参照"/"比照"已有规范、论证同案同判。 核心原则：相似案件相似处理（Like cases should be treated alike）。 禁用领域：刑法定罪量刑、税法课税要件、行政处罚裁量（严格限制类推）。 与deductive-reasoning互补：演绎推理是大前提明确时的推理，类比推理是大前提缺失时的推理。 ([vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal](https://github.com/vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal/tree/HEAD/plugins/legal-atomic/analogical-reasoning) · ★50)

Use when the user wants to systematically evaluate multiple competing explanations for what has happened, is happening, or is likely to happen. ACH overcomes Confirmation Bias by rejecting hypotheses rather than confirming them: the lead hypothesis has the fewest inconsistencies, not the most supporting evidence.
